# SI8 Intelligence — Digest Article Log

All articles surfaced by the weekly news digest, regardless of action taken.
**Auto-updated every Monday** by the digest script via GitHub Actions.

To mark an article as acted on (posted/doc updated), change `☐` → `☑` in the last column.

**Action key:** `post` = LinkedIn post · `update` = internal doc updated · `post+update` = both · `monitor` = no action needed
